# Skills · N · part 57

910 entries, notebook-rig to notes-and-roam-research. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **notebook-rig**: Save a pytweezer camera's frames into the zips of MOTMaster runs fired from a lab-book Jupyter notebook by pycaf, with PycafCamera (pytweezer/notebook/), keeping them readable by analysis3.readFromZip. Use whenever the user wants a pytweezer camera (Rb HamCam, CaF ThorCam, ORCA) to record during a pycaf scan or motmaster_single_run, asks why a run's zip has no or the wrong frames, or works on pytweezer/notebook/pycaf_camera.py, zips.py, drivers/shot_frames.py or the camera frame-index protocol in camera_base.py. ([ColdMatter/pytweezer](https://github.com/ColdMatter/pytweezer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/notebook-rig))
- **notebook-role-reuse**: Spawn an agent so it REUSES an existing Agent Notebook shelf (a "role" in the nb CLI's own terms) — loading that shelf's accumulated lessons at the start and writing new ones back to the same notebook before it returns. Use whenever you spawn a general-purpose (or named) subagent to do domain work other agents have done before: code review, repo mapping, patching, reproduction, design critique, and so on. Also use when you want the orchestrator to discover existing shelves and reuse them. ([kookr-ai/kookr](https://github.com/kookr-ai/kookr/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/notebook-role-reuse) · ★5)
- **notebook-rollup**: Generate weekly narrative rollups for the JD vault's agent notebook. Surveys `03.13 Agent notebook/YYYY-MM/` per-session notes, finds COMPLETED ISO weeks that have sessions but no rollup yet, and writes `rollups/Agent rollup for YYYY-WNN.md` for each — a hand-quality thematic synthesis. Never rolls up the in-progress week. Use to catch up the rollup layer (manually; there is no schedule). ([nelsonlove/cc-notebook](https://github.com/nelsonlove/cc-notebook/tree/HEAD/skills/notebook-rollup))
